# Current foundation audit status

Candidate source `e315b3a048fddd1729461fb92502f73b7d8e2b94`, evidence `b425324a7f0a8007`, branch `codex/save-lease-cleanup`. **Unpublished; the optimization/audit goal remains active.** Last publication retained by evidence is v51/place140, not a fresh Dashboard check.

Start with [the current handoff](../../chet/save-lease-cleanup.md). Named earlier-source receipts below remain historical unless explicitly verified on this candidate.

## Current verification

One production module changed since navigation startup: SaveSession now queues budgeted metadata-only cleanup after giving up an owned lease. A refused unseen/changed life remains protected, while a subsequent visit can acquire the released lock without waiting120 seconds. Atomic callbacks preserve another owner and the raw payload/checksum/time/backup/extension fields. Unknown or damaged progress is not rewritten. Backend failure can still leave the normal expiry fallback.

**1072 focused checks in five suites**,217/217 compilation, strict analysis and runner5 pass. Same expanded state suite: predecessor805 passed/26 failed, candidate831/0, including62 new assertions across9 cases. Source-bound production XML and rejection of the test artifact are verified. Windows31770 and Linux39811082793 are active on this exact source;65/443946 is expected, not a completed result. Native diagnostic is prepared with218 fingerprints and production Persistence disabled. Studio reported Low System Resources before the file was opened; it was declined and Studio exited, so no native cleanup result is claimed.

Predecessor navigation snapshotbc77830 passed65 suites/443884 on Windows and Linux,28 native startup assertions including the real40.014088-second timeout, and21 bounded gameplay replay projections/377 RNG draws at30/60/120Hz. Those receipts remain intact; they do not substitute for the changed candidate's full gates or broad device/performance/rejoin review.

## Next actions

1. Continue exact full Windows31770 and Linux39811082793; retain terminal outputs and verify againste315b3a. Do not restart after an observation timeout or mutate runtime/default tests while Windows bundles its suites.
2. Run the prepared9-case native cleanup diagnostic when Studio has enough resources. This uses synthetic MemoryBackend instances and native-captured rests; online client rejoin remains separate.
3. Finish the explicit displayed-rest/foreign-lease Continue protocol and lossless old-world restoration. Do not drop/relabel ecology, steal live locks, upload defaults after failed reads or silently treat a changed life as consented.
4. Continue representative profiling, physical input/collision/network/device/audio/accessibility review and broader moving/crossing/restored-life replay. Historical mixed performance experiments establish no causal FPS improvement. Publish only after the actual release gates are satisfied.
